Method: 构建了SFI-Bench基准，包含1500+专家标注问题，基于多样化第一视角室内视频扫描数据。系统评估两个维度：(1)结构化空间推理（理解复杂布局、形成空间表征）；(2)功能推理（推断物体可供性及其上下文依赖的效用）。任务类型包括条件计数、多跳关系推理、功能配对、知识引导的故障排除等。

Result: 当前多模态大模型在结合空间记忆与功能推理及外部知识方面持续表现不佳，揭示了实现具身智能的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: SFI-Bench可作为评估多模态智能体向更高认知能力和真正具身智能方向进展的诊断工具。

Abstract: Human-level agentic intelligence extends beyond low-level geometric perception, evolving from recognizing where things are to understanding what they are for. While existing benchmarks effectively evaluate the geometric perception capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), they fall short of probing the higher-order cognitive abilities required for grounded intelligence. To address this gap, we introduce the Spatial-Functional Intelligence Benchmark (SFI-Bench), a video-based benchmark with over 1,500 expert-annotated questions derived from diverse egocentric indoor video scans. SFI-Bench systematically evaluates two complementary dimensions of advanced reasoning: (1) Structured Spatial Reasoning, which requires understanding complex layouts and forming coherent spatial representations, and (2) Functional Reasoning, which involves inferring object affordances and their context-dependent utility. The benchmark includes tasks such as conditional counting, multi-hop relational reasoning, functional pairing, and knowledge-grounded troubleshooting, directly challenging models to integrate perception, memory, and inference. Our experiments reveal that current MLLMs consistently struggle to combine spatial memory with functional reasoning and external knowledge, highlighting a critical bottleneck in achieving grounded intelligence. SFI-Bench therefore provides a diagnostic tool for measuring progress toward more cognitively capable and truly grounded multimodal agents.

</details>


### [109] [Video Generation with Predictive Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02134)
*Yian Zhao,Feng Wang,Qiushan Guo,Chang Liu,Xiangyang Ji,Jian Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Predictive Video VAE (PV-VAE)，通过引入预测重建目标——随机丢弃未来帧并同时训练解码器重建过去帧和预测未来帧——来增强视频潜空间的扩散友好性。该模型在视频生成上收敛速度提升52%，FVD提升34.42，并展现出良好的可扩展性和下游视频理解增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VAE在重建质量上表现良好，但继续优化重建并不一定能提升生成性能。如何增强视频潜空间的"可扩散性"（diffusability）仍是一个关键且未解决的挑战。

Method: 受预测世界模型启发，提出预测重建目标：随机丢弃未来帧，仅编码部分过去观测帧，训练解码器同时重建已观测帧和预测未来帧。该设计迫使潜空间编码时序预测结构，形成对视频动态更连贯的理解。

Result: 在UCF101上，相较于Wan2.2 VAE，收敛速度提升52%，FVD提升34.42；潜空间有效捕捉时序连贯性和运动先验，且生成性能随VAE训练提升而改善，展现出良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: PV-VAE验证了预测学习能够有效提升视频潜空间的扩散友好性，不仅改善生成质量，还对下游视频理解任务有一致增益，表明其潜空间能更好地捕捉时序连贯性和运动先验。

Abstract: Video Variational Autoencoder (VAE) enables latent video generative modeling by mapping the visual world into compact spatiotemporal latent spaces, improving training efficiency and stability. While existing video VAEs achieve commendable reconstruction quality, continued optimization of reconstruction does not necessarily translate into improved generative performance. How to enhance the diffusability of video latents remains a critical and unresolved challenge. In this work, inspired by principles of predictive world modeling, we investigate the potential of predictive learning to improve the video generative modeling. To this end, we introduce a simple and effective predictive reconstruction objective that unifies predictive learning with video reconstruction. Specifically, we randomly discard future frames and encode only partial past observations, while training the decoder to reconstruct the observed frames and predict future ones simultaneously. This design encourages the latent space to encode temporally predictive structures and build a more coherent understanding of video dynamics, thereby improving generation quality. Our model, termed Predictive Video VAE (PV-VAE), achieves superior performance on video generation, with 52% faster convergence and a 34.42 FVD improvement over the Wan2.2 VAE on UCF101. Furthermore, comprehensive analyses demonstrate that PV-VAE not only exhibits favorable scalability, with generative performance improving alongside VAE training, but also yields consistent gains in downstream video understanding, underscoring a latent space that effectively captures temporal coherence and motion priors.

</details>


### [110] [SpecEdit: Training-Free Acceleration for Diffusion based Image Editing via Semantic Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02152)
*Zhengan Yan,Shikang Zheng,Haoran Qin,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Jiacheng Liu,Jiaxuan Ren,Yuqi Lin,Peiliang Cai,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpecEdit是一种无需训练的动态分辨率框架，通过草稿-验证机制，先在低分辨率下估计语义编辑结果，然后仅对编辑相关的token进行高分辨率去噪，实现最高10倍（Qwen-Image-Edit）和7倍（FLUX.1-Kontext-dev）加速，结合其他加速技术可达13倍加速，同时保持编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的图像编辑方法虽然语义可控性强，但计算成本高昂，因为需要在所有空间token上进行迭代的高分辨率去噪。现有的动态分辨率采样方法使用低级启发式方法（如边缘检测或通道方差）进行上采样，与编辑语义弱对齐，可能导致结构不一致；且空间区域常被上采样而未验证是否真的需要语义修改，导致冗余高分辨率计算和累积误差。

Method: SpecEdit采用草稿-验证（draft-and-verify）方案：首先在低分辨率下生成草稿，估计语义编辑结果；然后通过token级别的差异识别与编辑相关的token；仅对这些编辑相关token进行高分辨率去噪，其余token保持粗分辨率。

Result: 在Qwen-Image-Edit和FLUX.1-Kontext-dev上分别实现最高10倍和7倍加速，同时保持强编辑质量。与步蒸馏等其他加速技术互补，结合后可达13倍加速。

Conclusion: SpecEdit是一种训练无关、高效的动态分辨率图像编辑框架，能够显著加速扩散模型编辑过程而不牺牲质量，且可与其他加速方法叠加使用。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image editing offers strong semantic controllability, but remains computationally expensive due to iterative high-resolution denoising over all spatial tokens. Dynamic-resolution sampling reduces this cost by performing early steps at reduced resolution. However, existing approaches prioritize upsampling using low-level heuristics such as edge detection or channel variance, which are weakly aligned with editing semantics and may lead to structural inconsistency. Moreover, spatial regions are often upsampled without verifying whether semantic modification is actually required, resulting in redundant high-resolution computation and accumulated errors. Therefore, we propose SpecEdit, a training-free dynamic-resolution framework tailored for diffusion-based image editing. SpecEdit follows a draft-and-verify scheme: a low-resolution draft first estimates the semantic outcome, after which token-level discrepancies are used to identify edit-relevant tokens for high-resolution denoising, while the remaining tokens stay at a coarse resolution. Experiments on Qwen-Image-Edit and FLUX.1-Kontext-dev demonstrate up to 10x and 7x acceleration, while maintaining strong quality. SpecEdit is complementary to step distillation and other acceleration techniques, achieving up to 13x speedup when combined with existing methods. Our code is in supplementary material and will be released on GitHub.

</details>


### [111] [Cross-Polarization Fusion of VV AND VH SAR Observations for Improved Flood Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02153)
*Jagrati Talreja,Tewodros Syum Gebre,Leila Hashemi Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了VV和VH交叉极化SAR数据融合在洪水制图中的应用，采用深度学习分割框架，实验表明VV-VH融合在植被覆盖和异质洪水区域中优于单极化模型，能更准确地描绘洪水边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单极化SAR数据在复杂环境（如表面散射与体散射共存区域）中进行洪水制图仍存在挑战，需探索交叉极化融合以提高制图精度。

Method: 采用深度学习分割框架，联合利用VV和VH极化信息的互补特性，并在相同训练条件下比较三种输入配置（VV only、VH only、VV-VH融合）。

Result: VV-VH融合模型在IoU和F1-score等指标上始终优于单极化模型，尤其在植被覆盖和异质洪水区域表现更优，边界 delineation 更准确。

Conclusion: 交叉极化SAR融合对提升SAR洪水制图的可靠性至关重要，在灾害监测应用中具有显著价值。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery is widely used for flood monitoring due to its all-weather and day-night imaging capability. However, flood mapping using single-polarization SAR data remains challenging in complex environments where surface and volume scattering coexist. In this paper, we investigate the effectiveness of cross-polarization fusion of VV and VH SAR observations for improved flood mapping. A deep learning-based segmentation framework is employed to jointly exploit complementary information from VV and VH polarizations. To ensure a fair evaluation, three configurations are compared under identical training conditions: VV only, VH only, and fused VV-VH input. Performance is assessed using standard flood mapping metrics, including Intersection over Union (IoU) and F1-score, along with qualitative visual analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that VV-VH fusion consistently outperforms single-polarization models, particularly in vegetated and heterogeneous flood regions, leading to more accurate flood boundary delineation. The findings highlight the importance of cross-polarization SAR fusion for enhancing the reliability of SAR-based flood mapping in disaster monitoring applications.

</details>


### [112] [Heterogeneous Model Fusion for Privacy-Aware Multi-Camera Surveillance via Synthetic Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02169)
*Peggy Joy Lu,Wei-Yu Chen,Yao-Tsung Huang,Vincent Shin-Mu Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeroCrystal是一个面向多摄像头域自适应目标检测的隐私保护框架，通过生成式数据增强（单样本扩散模型）、联邦学习（概率Faster R-CNN+动态模型对比策略）和知识蒸馏（不一致类别整合算法）三阶段，在保护数据隐私、处理类别不平衡和异构架构的条件下，实现了mAP 33.4%的新SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多摄像头域自适应目标检测中存在三大问题：1) 数据隐私风险（传统方法需聚合大量目标域图像）；2) 类别不平衡导致的长尾退化；3) 客户端间异构架构难以统一联邦学习。亟需一种能同时解决隐私、类别分布和架构异质性的统一框架。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) Generated Stage：基于单张目标域图像的扩散生成模块，通过提示控制合成特定目标实例，实现隐私保护的数据增强和可控稀有对象生成；2) Federated Stage：客户端采用概率Faster R-CNN提升定位精度，动态模型对比策略抑制域特定偏差，服务端实现异构模型融合；3) Distilled Stage：不一致类别整合算法解决标签不一致和架构异构问题。

Result: 在多个跨域检测基准上，HeroCrystal在隐私保护设定下mAP提升+2.1%，达到新的SOTA 33.4% mAP，优于现有多源域适应和联邦学习基线方法。

Conclusion: HeroCrystal通过生成式隐私增强、联邦异构融合和类别一致性蒸馏三大模块协同，有效解决了多摄像头监控场景下的域自适应检测难题，为实际AI监控系统的隐私合规部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We propose HeroCrystal, a novel privacy-preserving framework for multi-camera domain-adaptive object detection, addressing challenges such as data privacy, class imbalance, and heterogeneous architectures. Our framework consists of three key stages. In the Generated Stage, we introduce a one-shot, target-aware diffusion-based generation module that learns visual style from a single target-domain image while leveraging prompt-based control to synthesize specific object instances. Unlike conventional style transfer-based methods that require large target datasets and ignore semantic-level discrepancies, our approach enables privacy-preserving augmentation to reduce ethical concerns, and introduces controllable rare object generation to mitigate long-tailed category degradation. In the Federated Stage, we employ probabilistic Faster R-CNN on the client side to improve localization accuracy, and a dynamic model contrastive strategy to suppress domain-specific bias. The server side performs model fusion across heterogeneous architectures without accessing raw data. Finally, in the Distilled Stage, we propose an inconsistent categories integration algorithm to resolve label inconsistency and architecture heterogeneity across clients. Extensive experiments on multiple cross-domain detection benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing multi-source domain adaptation and federated learning baselines under multi-class, privacy-preserving settings. Our method improves mAP by +2.1% over prior privacy-preserving approaches and achieves a new state-of-the-art mAP of 33.4%, highlighting the effectiveness of HeroCrystal in enabling practical multi-camera AI surveillance systems.

</details>


### [113] [SlimDiffSR: Toward Lightweight and Efficient Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution via Diffusion Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02198)
*Ce Wang,Zhenyu Hu,Wanjie Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlimDiffSR，一种轻量级扩散模型框架用于遥感图像超分辨率，通过不确定性引导的时间步分配策略、结构化剪枝和MMD蒸馏，实现高达200倍推理加速和20倍参数压缩，同时保持与多步扩散模型相当的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像超分辨率中表现优异，但其高计算成本限制了在遥感应用中的实际部署，亟需轻量化方案。

Method: 1) 不确定性引导的时间步分配策略，将重建难度与扩散时间步关联，构建更强的单步教师模型；2) 面向遥感图像的结构化剪枝策略，包含频率可分离卷积、方向可分离卷积和查询驱动的全局聚合模块；3) 在蒸馏过程中引入最大均值差异(MMD)对齐师生模型的特征分布。

Result: 与多步扩散模型相比，实现高达200倍推理加速和20倍参数量缩减，在多个遥感基准上取得效率与重建质量的良好平衡，优于现有轻量扩散基线。

Conclusion: SlimDiffSR有效解决了扩散模型在遥感超分辨率中的计算瓶颈，验证了针对遥感数据特性设计轻量扩散范式的可行性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently achieved remarkable performance in image super-resolution (SR), but their high computational cost limits practical deployment in remote sensing applications. To address this issue, we propose SlimDiffSR, a lightweight and efficient diffusion-based framework for real-world remote sensing image super-resolution. Unlike existing single-step diffusion methods that rely on fixed timesteps, we first introduce an uncertainty-guided timestep assignment strategy to construct a stronger single-step teacher model, where reconstruction difficulty is explicitly linked to diffusion timesteps, enabling adaptive generative strength. Building upon this teacher, we further present a structured pruning strategy tailored to remote sensing imagery, which systematically removes redundant semantic modules and replaces standard operations with lightweight designs, including frequency-separable convolution, direction-separable convolution, and a query-driven global aggregation module. These components explicitly exploit the unique characteristics of remote sensing data, such as sparse high-frequency details, strong directional patterns, and long-range spatial dependencies. To enhance knowledge transfer, we incorporate Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) into the distillation process to align feature distributions between the teacher and student models. Extensive experiments on multiple remote sensing benchmarks demonstrate that SlimDiffSR achieves a favorable balance between efficiency and reconstruction quality. In particular, it attains up to $200\times$ inference acceleration and a $20\times$ reduction in model parameters compared with multi-step diffusion models, while achieving competitive perceptual quality and clearly outperforming existing lightweight diffusion baselines in efficiency. The code is available at: https://github.com/wwangcece/SlimDiffSR.

</details>


### [114] [Super-resolution of airborne laser scanning point clouds for forest inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02201)
*Jinyuan Shao,Sangyoong Park,Chunxi Zhao,Ayman Habib,Songlin Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DFSR模型，通过深度学习提升ALS森林点云密度并降低噪声，使森林调查（树干检测、DBH测量、树干重建）精度大幅提升，F1从0.71提升至0.97，DBH的RMSE从13.45 cm降至6.43 cm，且可跨平台泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS点云稀疏且噪声大，导致单木级森林调查（如树干定位、树木尺寸估计）不准确，需要提升点云质量和密度。

Method: 提出3DFSR，一种基于体素的U-Net架构CNN模型，同时提升点密度和降噪。

Result: 在温带和寒带森林ALS数据上，3DFSR在Chamfer Distance（0.249 m）和Hausdorff Distance（2.711 m）上优于现有方法；树干检测F1从0.71提升至0.97，DBH估计RMSE从13.45 cm降至6.43 cm，树干重建与MLS相当（Chamfer距离0.170 m，R²=0.95）；适用于10-1700点/m²密度范围，可跨平台泛化。

Conclusion: 3DFSR能有效将低质量ALS点云增强至接近TLS/MLS质量，使传统针对高密度点云的森林调查算法可直接应用，显著提升森林调查精度。

Abstract: Airborne Laser Scanning (ALS) can collect point clouds across large areas, enabling large-scale forest inventory. However, ALS point clouds are sparse and noisy, resulting in inaccurate individual-tree-level forest inventory, such as stem localization and tree size estimation. To overcome this problem, we propose a deep learning model, 3D Forest Super Resolution (3DFSR), to simultaneously improve point density and reduce noise for ALS forest point cloud. 3DFSR is a voxel-based CNN with a U-Net architecture. The proposed 3DFSR is evaluated on ALS point clouds collected in both temperate forests in the U.S. and boreal forests in Germany. Experimental results demonstrate that 3DFSR can generate finer point clouds of tree structure than other state-of-the-art point cloud super-resolution algorithms, achieving 0.249 m Chamfer Distance and 2.711 m Hausdorff Distance. Furthermore, to verify the effectiveness of 3DFSR point clouds in forest inventory, we conduct stem detection, DBH measurements, and stem reconstruction on both original ALS point clouds and 3DFSR enhanced point clouds. We find that stem detection and reconstruction algorithms developed for TLS/MLS point clouds can directly work on our 3DFSR point clouds, and DBH can be derived with circle-fitting method. F1 score of stem detection is improved from 0.71 on original ALS point clouds to 0.97 on 3DFSR point clouds; DBH estimation improves from 13.45 cm RMSE using allometric equations to 6.43 cm using circle fitting; comparing to stems reconstruction from MLS point clouds, stem reconstructed from 3DFSR point clouds has 0.170 m of Chamfer Distance and 0.377 m of Hausdorff Distance, and 0.95 R2 volume estimation. Finally, we find that the proposed 3DFSR is applicable to process point densities from 10 to 1700 points/m2; it also can be generalized across data collected from different LiDAR platforms without transfer learning.

</details>


### [115] [MultiSense-Pneumo: A Multimodal Learning Framework for Pneumonia Screening in Resource-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02207)
*Dineth Jayakody,Pasindu Thenahandi,Chameli Dommanige*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSense-Pneumo，一个集成症状描述、咳嗽音频、语音语言和胸部X光片的多模态肺炎筛查与分诊支持框架，可在低算力设备上离线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺炎在全球尤其是资源匮乏地区造成严重疾病负担，现有计算方法多为单模态且主要依赖影像，缺乏融合多种临床信号（症状、音频、影像）的轻量级多模态筛查方案。

Method: 该系统整合了确定性症状分诊、基于LightGBM的声学分类、使用ResNet-18的域对抗X光片分析、基于Transformer的语音识别，以及可解释的多模态融合算子，将各模态转化为归一化风险信号并聚合成统一的筛查估计。

Result: 实验表明影像路径在域偏移下具有鲁棒性，但声学信号在少数类召回率方面存在局限性。

Conclusion: MultiSense-Pneumo被定位为用于筛查和分诊支持的研究原型，而非经临床验证的诊断系统，适合社区健康工作者、乡村诊所和应急响应场景。

Abstract: Pneumonia remains a leading global cause of morbidity and mortality, particularly in low resource settings where access to imaging, laboratory testing, and specialist care is limited. Clinical assessment relies on heterogeneous evidence, including symptoms, respiratory patterns, and chest imaging, making screening inherently multimodal. However, many existing computational approaches remain unimodal and focus primarily on radiographs. In this work, we present MultiSense-Pneumo, a multimodal framework for pneumonia oriented screening and triage support that integrates structured symptom descriptors, cough audio, spoken language, and chest radiographs. The system combines deterministic symptom triage, LightGBM based acoustic classification, domain adversarial radiograph analysis using ResNet 18, transformer based speech recognition, and an interpretable multimodal fusion operator. Each modality is transformed into a normalized risk signal and aggregated into a unified screening estimate, enabling transparent and modular decision support. MultiSense-Pneumo is designed for real world deployment under modest computational constraints and can operate fully offline on standard laptop class hardware, making it suitable for community health workers, rural clinics, and emergency response settings. Experimental results demonstrate robustness of the radiograph pathway under domain shifts, while highlighting limitations in minority class recall for acoustic signals. MultiSense-Pneumo is intended as a research prototype for screening and triage support rather than a clinically validated diagnostic system.

</details>


### [116] [InfiltrNet: Dual-Branch CNN-Transformer Architecture for Brain Tumor Infiltration Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02230)
*S M Asif Hossain,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InfiltrNet，一种结合CNN和Swin Transformer的双分支架构，通过交叉注意力融合模块从多模态MRI预测胶质瘤的三区域浸润风险图。采用基于距离变换的标签生成策略从BraTS标注导出浸润风险区，并使用结合Dice-CrossEntropy和边界感知损失的训练策略。在BraTS 2020和BraTS 2025上的实验表明InfiltrNet优于五种基线方法，可解释性分析验证了模型关注临床相关的瘤周区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤会浸润至MRI可见肿瘤边界以外的组织，现有深度学习方法仅分割可见肿瘤，未能估计周围组织的浸润风险，而这对手术规划和放疗至关重要。

Method: 提出InfiltrNet双分支架构，包含CNN编码器和Swin Transformer编码器，通过交叉注意力融合模块结合；采用基于距离变换的标签生成策略从BraTS标注创建三区域浸润风险图；使用Dice-CrossEntropy和边界感知损失的联合训练，并在中间解码层添加辅助监督头。

Result: 在BraTS 2020和BraTS 2025数据集上，InfiltrNet优于五种基线方法；GradCAM++和Occlusion敏感性分析表明模型关注临床相关的瘤周区域。

Conclusion: InfiltrNet能够有效预测胶质瘤周围组织的浸润风险，为手术和放疗规划提供更有价值的空间信息，且模型决策具有临床可解释性。

Abstract: Gliomas are aggressive brain tumors that infiltrate surrounding tissue beyond the visible tumor margins observed on Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI). Predicting the spatial extent of this infiltration is essential for surgical planning and radiation therapy, yet existing deep learning approaches focus on segmenting the visible tumor rather than estimating infiltration risk in the surrounding tissue. This paper presents InfiltrNet, a novel dual-branch architecture that combines a convolutional neural network (CNN) encoder with a Swin Transformer encoder through cross-attention fusion modules to predict three-zone infiltration risk maps from multimodal MRI. A label generation strategy based on distance transforms is proposed to derive reproducible infiltration risk zones from standard Brain Tumor Segmentation (BraTS) annotations. InfiltrNet is trained with a combined Dice-CrossEntropy and boundary-aware loss augmented by auxiliary supervision heads at intermediate decoder levels. Extensive experiments on BraTS 2020 and BraTS 2025 demonstrate that InfiltrNet outperforms five established baselines. Explainability analysis using GradCAM++ and Occlusion sensitivity confirms that the model attends to clinically relevant peritumoral regions.

</details>


### [117] [Fine-Tuning Impairs the Balancedness of Foundation Models in Long-tailed Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02247)
*Shihao Hou,Chikai Shang,Zhiheng Yang,Jiacheng Yang,Xinyi Shang,Junlong Gao,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPuReL方法，通过梯度纯化和残差学习解决个性化联邦学习中非独立同分布数据与长尾类分布共存的问题，在保持全局模型类平衡的同时实现无偏个性化，实验表明该方法在多种长尾场景下均优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中个性化联邦学习常面临非独立同分布数据与长尾类分布共存的问题，现有方法存在两个局限：(i)微调会侵蚀基础模型固有的类平衡性，导致性能低于零样本基线；(ii)传统个性化技术通过参数或特征融合将偏差传递给局部模型。这些问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出联邦学习梯度纯化与残差学习(FedPuReL)方法：(i)利用零样本预测纯化局部梯度，维护类平衡的全局模型；(ii)将个性化建模为冻结全局模型之上的残差校正，从而实现无偏个性化。

Result: 大量实验表明，FedPuReL在多种长尾场景下始终优于现有最优方法，在全局模型和个性化模型上均取得了更优性能。

Conclusion: FedPuReL通过梯度纯化保留全局模型的平衡知识，并结合残差学习实现无偏个性化，有效解决了长尾分布下的个性化联邦学习挑战。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) with foundation models has emerged as a promising paradigm enabling clients to adapt to heterogeneous data distributions. However, real-world scenarios often face the co-occurrence of non-IID data and long-tailed class distributions, presenting unique challenges that remain underexplored in PFL. In this paper, we investigate this long-tailed personalized federated learning and observe that current methods suffer from two limitations: (i) fine-tuning degrades performance below zero-shot baselines due to the erosion of inherent class balance in foundation models; (ii) conventional personalization techniques further transfer this bias to local models through parameter or feature-level fusion. To address these challenges, we propose Federated Learning via Gradient Purification and Residual Learning (FedPuReL), which preserves balanced knowledge in the global model while enabling unbiased personalization. Specifically, we purify local gradients using zero-shot predictions to maintain a class-balanced global model, and model personalization as residual correction atop the frozen global model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedPuReL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior performance on both global and personalized models across diverse long-tailed scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/shihaohou/FedPuReL.

</details>


### [118] [Rethinking Electro-Optical Vision Foundation Models for Remote Sensing Retrieval: A Controlled Comparison with Generalist VFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02283)
*Hyobin Park,Minseok Seo,Dong-Geol Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控对比实验发现，强通用视觉基础模型在遥感图像检索任务上能够与专用遥感基础模型竞争甚至超越后者，且通用模型在跨场景泛化方面表现更稳定，而专用模型在跨场景评估中性能显著下降。研究表明，仅依靠遥感预训练并不能保证更强的检索导向表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域数据获取成本高、标注依赖专家知识，视觉基础模型利用大规模无标注数据的能力在此场景尤为重要。然而，现有的遥感专用视觉基础模型是否真的优于强通用视觉基础模型尚不清楚，特别是在检索任务中缺乏受控对比研究。

Method: 在相同数据集、检索协议和评估指标下，对代表性的遥感专用视觉基础模型和通用视觉基础模型进行受控对比，评估域内性能和跨场景泛化能力。

Result: 强通用视觉基础模型在遥感图像检索中与遥感专用模型相当甚至更优；专用模型在跨场景评估中性能显著下降，而通用模型迁移更稳定。

Conclusion: 仅靠遥感数据预训练不能保证更强的检索表征，当前遥感专用预训练策略存在局限性，未来遥感视觉基础模型需要更好地利用遥感图像的物理、空间、光谱和地理特性。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have attracted significant attention for their ability to leverage large-scale unlabeled visual data. This advantage is particularly important in remote sensing, where data acquisition is costly and annotation often requires expert knowledge. Recent electro-optical vision foundation models aim to learn domain-specific representations from remote sensing imagery, but it remains unclear whether they are more effective than strong generalist vision foundation models under retrieval-based evaluation. In this study, we conduct a controlled comparison between representative EO-specific and generalist vision foundation models for remote sensing image retrieval. Using the same datasets, retrieval protocol, and evaluation metric, we evaluate both in-domain performance and cross-scene generalization. Our results show that strong generalist vision foundation models are competitive with, and in some cases outperform, existing EO-specific models. Moreover, EO-specific models often suffer from substantial degradation under cross-scene evaluation, while generalist models show more stable transfer. These findings suggest that EO pretraining alone does not guarantee stronger retrieval-oriented remote sensing representations. We discuss the limitations of current EO-specific pretraining strategies and highlight the need for future EO vision foundation models to better exploit the physical, spatial, spectral, and geographic characteristics of remote sensing imagery.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Known Objects: A Novel Framework for Open-Set Object Detection using Negative-Aware Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02284)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yao Lu,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NAN-SPOT框架，无需重训练基础检测器，通过负感知范数（NAN）隐层度量快速估计物体性，仅需数百张图像和数分钟训练，在OSOD任务上超越需要大量训练的方法，并构建了更完备的COCO-Open数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集目标检测方法通常需要大量重训练检测器来学习物体性（objectness），成本高昂。作者假设标准预训练检测器已在大量已知类别训练中隐含了物体性线索，从而探索能否以极轻量方式直接利用这些线索。

Method: 提出NAN-SPOT框架：无需重训练基础检测器，利用隐藏层中的负感知范数（Negative-Aware Norm, NAN）作为物体性度量，仅在数百张图像上微调数分钟即可完成。

Result: 在未知目标检测上性能优于需要大量训练的方法，且不影响已知目标检测性能。同时构建了COCO-Open数据集，将未知目标标注从433个扩展至1853个。

Conclusion: NAN-SPOT在效率和鲁棒性上具有优势，是迈向自动驾驶开放世界感知的有前景的一步。

Abstract: Open-Set Object Detection (OSOD) is crucial for autonomous driving, where perception systems must recognize and localize both known and previously unseen objects in complex, dynamic environments. While recent approaches deliver promising results, they often require retraining the detector extensively to learn objectness, which describes the likelihood that a bounding box tightly encloses a valid object, regardless of whether its category was learned during training. Deviating from existing work, we hypothesize that standard off-the-shelf detectors may already contain helpful cues for objectness, owing to their training on numerous and diverse known categories. Building on this idea, we propose NAN-SPOT, a training-light framework that does not require to retrain the base object detector and estimates objectness by leveraging a hidden layer metric called Negative-Aware Norm (NAN), requiring only minutes of training on just hundreds of images. To support comprehensive evaluation, we introduce COCO-Open, an expanded version of the existing COCO-Mixed dataset, increasing unknown object annotations from 433 to 1853, making it the most exhaustively labeled dataset for OSOD to the best of our knowledge. Experimental results demonstrate that NAN-SPOT achieves even better performance on unknown object detection than methods requiring heavy training, without compromising performance on known objects. This efficiency and robustness make NAN-SPOT a promising step towards open-world perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [120] [LabBuilder: Protocol-Grounded 3D Layout Generation for Interactable and Safe Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02288)
*Jianbao Cao,Zhangrui Zhao,Bohan Feng,Zixuan Hu,Rui Li,Haiyuan Wan,Chenxi Li,Jingyuan Li,Wenzhe Cai,Lei Bai,Wanli Ouyang,Lingyu Duan,Di Huang,Mingting Pan,Sha Zhang,Xinzhu Ma,Shixiang Tang,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LabBuilder是一个端到端系统，能从文本描述自动生成并验证3D实验室布局，通过资产整理、约束优化和基准评估三个组件，生成比其他方法更真实、功能有效且安全的实验室环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化实验室的部署受限于安全可执行环境的设计困难，现有3D场景生成方法主要面向家庭场景，仅优化视觉合理性，忽略了科学实验所需的严格功能语义和安全约束。

Method: LabBuilder包含三个紧密耦合组件：LabForge整理带标注的资产元数据集和化学知识，将自然语言规范翻译为结构化协议；LabGen基于这些协议通过迭代的约束感知优化策略合成实验室布局；LabTouchstone评估生成的布局作为统一基准。

Result: 大量实验表明，LabBuilder显著优于现有最先进方法，生成的实验室环境不仅真实，而且功能有效且安全，适用于复杂实验工作流程。

Conclusion: LabBuilder成功解决了科学实验场景的自动化布局生成问题，在功能语义和安全性方面超越了传统面向家庭场景的3D生成方法，为自动化实验室的部署提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated laboratories hold the promise of accelerating scientific discovery, yet their deployment is bottlenecked by the difficulty of designing safe and executable environments. While simulator-based design offers scalability, existing 3D scene generation methods are primarily tailored for household settings, optimizing for visual plausibility while neglecting the rigorous functional semantics and safety constraints essential for scientific experimentation. We present LabBuilder, an end-to-end system that generates and verifies 3D laboratory layouts from concise textual specifications. It operates through three tightly coupled components: LabForge first curates a meta-dataset of annotated assets and chemical knowledge, translating natural language specifications into structured protocols; building on these protocols, LabGen synthesizes laboratory layouts via an iterative, constraint-aware optimization strategy; finally, LabTouchstone evaluates the resulting layouts as a unified benchmark. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LabBuilder significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, producing laboratory environments that are not only realistic but also functionally valid and safe for complex experimental workflows.

</details>


### [121] [A Hybrid Approach for Closing the Sim2real Appearance Gap in Game Engine Synthetic Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02291)
*Stefanos Pasios*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用扩散模型（FLUX.2-4B Klein）和图像到图像翻译模型（REGEN）提升合成数据集真实感的方法，并提出一种结合两者优势的混合方法，实验表明混合方法在保持语义一致性的前提下实现了更好的视觉真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏引擎虽通过光线追踪等技术显著提升了视觉保真度，但合成图像与现实世界图像之间仍存在明显的sim2real外观差距，限制了合成数据集在现实世界应用中的使用。

Method: 对比了最先进的图像生成与编辑扩散模型FLUX.2-4B Klein与传统图像到图像翻译模型REGEN在增强合成数据集真实感方面的性能，并提出了一种混合方法，将扩散模型强大的几何与材质变换能力与图像到图像翻译技术的分布匹配能力相结合。

Result: REGEN模型表现优于FLUX.2-4B Klein模型；将FLUX.2-4B Klein与REGEN模型结合使用，比单独使用任一模型能获得更好的视觉真实感，同时保持语义一致性。

Conclusion: 混合方法能够有效弥合sim2real外观差距，在提升合成数据真实感方面具有优势，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video game engines have been an important source for generating large volumes of visual synthetic datasets for training and evaluating computer vision algorithms that are to be deployed in the real world. While the visual fidelity of modern game engines has been significantly improved with technologies such as ray-tracing, a notable sim2real appearance gap between the synthetic and the real-world images still remains, which limits the utilization of synthetic datasets in real-world applications. In this letter, we investigate the ability of a state-of-the-art image generation and editing diffusion model (FLUX.2-4B Klein) to enhance the photorealism of synthetic datasets and compare its performance against a traditional image-to-image translation model (REGEN). Furthermore, we propose a hybrid approach that combines the strong geometry and material transformations of diffusion-based methods with the distribution-matching capabilities of image-to-image translation techniques. Through experiments, it is demonstrated that REGEN outperforms FLUX.2-4B Klein and that by combining both FLUX.2-4B Klein and REGEN models, better visual realism can be achieved compared to using each model individually, while maintaining semantic consistency. The code is available at: https://github.com/stefanos50/Hybrid-Sim2Real

</details>


### [122] [Improving Imbalanced Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Diagnosis via CBAM-Enhanced CNN Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02328)
*Duy Nguyen Huu,Duy Hoang Khuong,Ngu Huynh Cong Viet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将CBAM集成到传统CNN模块中，以增强胸部X光片多标签分类性能，在ChestXray14数据集上达到0.8695的平均AUC，优于多个现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片诊断耗时且依赖专家经验，深度学习虽提升了诊断效率，但仍面临类别不平衡和多病灶共存定位等问题。

Method: 受CBAM在特征精炼方面的优势和CNN在特征提取方面的能力启发，提出将CBAM集成到传统CNN模块中的策略，用于多标签分类任务。

Result: 在ChestXray14数据集上实现平均AUC达0.8695，优于多项现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的CBAM增强CNN策略能有效提升胸部X光片多标签分类性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Chest radiography is a widely used imaging modality for thoracic disease diagnosis, yet its conventional interpretation remains time-consuming and heavily dependent on expert knowledge. While deep learning has improved diagnostic efficiency through automated feature extraction, challenges such as class imbalance and the localization of multiple co-existing pathologies remain unsolved. In this paper, inspired by the strength of Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) in feature refinement and the capability of CNN blocks in feature extraction, we propose a strategy to integrate CBAM into traditional CNN blocks to enhance performance in multi-label classification tasks. Our method achieves a mean AUC of 0.8695 on ChestXray14 dataset, outperforming several state-of-the-art baselines.Our source code is available at: https://github.com/NNNguyenDuyyy/FETC_CBAM_Enhanced_CNN.git

</details>


### [123] [Enhancing Multimodal In-Context Learning via Inductive-Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02378)
*Haoyu Wang,Haonan Wang,Yuyan Chen,Jun Chen,Gang Liu,Qian Wang,Jiahong Yan,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态上下文学习（ICL）中的归纳鸿沟问题：视觉语言模型常通过错误推理得出正确答案，且难以从示范中提取一致规则。作者提出一个框架，包含基于相似性的视觉令牌压缩、动态注意力再平衡和思维链范式，将多模态ICL重构为原则性的归纳-演绎过程。结合监督微调与基于可验证奖励的强化学习辅助训练，在8个基准测试中显著优于标准ICL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态上下文学习存在归纳鸿沟：模型常基于错误推理得出正确结果，且难以从示范中提取一致规则。该问题被两个视觉层面的障碍进一步加剧：冗余视觉令牌掩盖文本线索、注意力分布偏向前置图像而忽视后续上下文。

Method: 提出一个归纳-演绎框架：(1)基于相似性的视觉令牌压缩模块过滤冗余图像块；(2)动态注意力再平衡机制公平分配所有图像的注意力；(3)思维链范式引导模型分析示例、推导规则并应用于查询。辅助训练结合监督微调与基于可验证奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在8个基准测试（涵盖视觉感知、逻辑推理、STEM问题、讽刺检测）中，多个开源视觉语言模型均获得一致且显著的性能提升，优于标准ICL基线。

Conclusion: 多模态ICL的归纳鸿沟可以通过结构化的归纳-演绎框架得到有效缓解，使模型具备真正的归纳能力。视觉令牌压缩和注意力再平衡是提升多模态ICL效果的关键组件。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) allows large models to adapt to tasks using a few examples, yet its extension to vision-language models (VLMs) remains fragile. Our analysis reveals that the fundamental limitation lies in an inductive gap, models often produce correct answers from flawed reasoning, while struggling to extract consistent rules across demonstrations. This gap is further exacerbated by two visual-level obstacles: an overwhelming proportion of redundant visual tokens that obscure textual cues, and a skewed attention distribution that favors the initial image at the expense of subsequent context. To address these issues, we introduce a framework that restructures multimodal ICL as a principled inductive-deductive process. The framework incorporates a similarity-based visual token compression module to filter out redundant patches, a dynamic attention rebalancing mechanism to distribute focus equitably across all images, and a chain-of-thought paradigm that explicitly guides the model to analyze individual examples, derive a generalizable rule, and then apply it to the query. An auxiliary learning pipeline combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards to reinforce faithful citation and noise filtering. Evaluations across eight benchmarks covering visual perception, logical reasoning, STEM problems, and sarcasm detection demonstrate consistent and significant improvements over standard ICL baselines for multiple open-source VLMs, highlighting the potential of equipping models with genuine inductive capabilities in multimodal settings.

</details>


### [124] [UnGAP: Uncertainty-Guided Affine Prompting for Real-Time Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02380)
*Conghui Li,Huanyu He,Xin Wang,Weiyao Lin,Chern Hong Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnGAP框架通过将异方差不确定性作为主动视觉提示反馈到特征学习中，解决了裂纹分割中因光照、模糊和纹理变化导致的偶然不确定性及异方差优化病态问题，实现了高精度实时分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不确定性感知方法仅将不确定性估计作为后验分析的被动端点，未能形成闭环以改进特征表示。裂纹分割依赖局部梯度而非全局语义一致性，独立像素级异方差建模最为适合，但这导致高预测方差削弱损失梯度，使模型忽略难样本并欠拟合复杂边界。

Method: 提出UnGAP框架，核心是不确定性提示特征调制器(UPFM)，将偶然不确定性作为主动视觉提示，通过逐像素仿射变换动态校准特征分布；同时引入边界感知检测头进一步约束预测精度，形成不确定性估计与特征学习之间的闭环机制。

Result: 大量实验表明UnGAP在保持实时推理速度的同时实现了优越的分割精度，验证了将不确定性从被动度量转化为主动校准工具的有效性。

Conclusion: UnGAP通过将不确定性作为主动校准信号而非被动指标，有效解决了异方差建模的病态优化问题，为结构健康监测中的实时裂纹分割提供了新范式。

Abstract: Real-time crack segmentation is vital for structural health monitoring but is plagued by aleatoric uncertainties arising from varying lighting, blur, and texture ambiguity. Current uncertainty-aware approaches typically treat uncertainty estimation as a passive endpoint for post-hoc analysis, failing to close the loop by feeding this information back to refine feature representations. We contend that independent pixel-wise heteroscedastic modeling is uniquely suited for crack segmentation, as cracks are defined by fine-grained local gradients rather than the global semantic coherence relied upon in general object segmentation. However, this approach suffers from a structural optimization pathology: high predicted variance attenuates loss gradients, effectively causing the model to ignore difficult samples and under-fit complex boundaries. To address these challenges, we propose UnGAP, a novel framework that establishes a closed-loop mechanism between uncertainty estimation and feature learning. Central to our approach is the Uncertainty-Prompted Feature Modulator (UPFM), which treats aleatoric uncertainty as an active visual prompt rather than a mere output. UPFM dynamically calibrates feature distributions through pixel-wise affine transformations. Crucially, this mechanism mitigates the heteroscedastic pathology by transforming high variance, which would otherwise indicate gradient suppression, into a constructive signal for stronger feature rectification in ambiguous regions. Additionally, a boundary-aware detection head is introduced to further constrain prediction precision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UnGAP balances superior segmentation accuracy with real-time inference speed, effectively validating the benefit of transforming uncertainty from a passive metric into an active calibration tool.

</details>


### [125] [FEAT: Fashion Editing and Try-On from Any Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02393)
*Soye Kwon,Keonyoung Lee,Dahuin Jung,Jaekoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEAT是一种从任意设计源进行服装编辑和虚拟试穿的方法，通过解耦双重注入（DDI）和正交引导噪声融合（OGNF）技术，支持服装和配饰的编辑与试穿，在设计灵活性、提示一致性和视觉真实性方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限于服装相关图像作为设计源，无法利用艺术品、抽象图像、自然照片等创意设计来源，且不支持包含配饰的完整着装。

Method: 提出解耦双重注入（DDI），分别处理服装类和非服装类设计源，通过内容和风格解耦选择性注入设计线索；提出正交引导噪声融合（OGNF），通过正交投影去除残留衣物并应用区域特定噪声策略，实现服装和配饰的虚拟试穿。

Result: 在设计灵活性、提示一致性和视觉真实性方面达到最优性能。

Conclusion: FEAT成功突破设计源限制，支持从任意设计源进行完整的服装和配饰编辑与虚拟试穿，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Fashion design aims to express a designer's creative intent and to depict how garments interact with the human body. Recent methods condition on multimodal inputs to support garment editing and virtual try-on. However, existing methods still (i) confine design to garment-related images, excluding creative design sources such as artwork, abstract imagery, and natural photographs, and (ii) cannot support complete outfits, including accessories. We present FEAT (Fashion Editing And Try-On from Any Design), a method that enables editing and try-on across garments and accessories using diverse design sources. To achieve this, we introduce Disentangled Dual Injection (DDI). It takes both apparel and non-apparel design sources and selectively injects design cues via content and style disentanglement. Furthermore, we propose Orthogonal-Guided Noise Fusion (OGNF), a training-free mechanism that removes residual garments via orthogonal projection and applies region-specific noise strategies to enable virtual try-on for both garments and accessories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FEAT achieves state-of-the-art performance in design flexibility, prompt consistency, and visual realism.

</details>


### [126] [DirectEdit: Step-Level Accurate Inversion for Flow-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02417)
*Desong Yang,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DirectEdit，一种基于流变换器的无需训练的图像编辑方法，通过直接对齐前向路径消除重建误差，无需额外NFE，并引入注意力特征注入和多分支掩码引导的噪声混合机制来平衡保真度与可编辑性，在多种场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练T2I模型的免训练图像编辑方法在反转过程中使用不匹配时间步的噪声潜变量近似重建路径，导致累积漂移，从根本上限制了重建保真度。

Method: 系统分析流变换器中的反转过程，提出DirectEdit：1) 直接对齐前向路径而非修正反转路径，消除固有重建误差且无需额外NFE；2) 引入基于注意力特征注入的保留机制；3) 多分支掩码引导的噪声混合策略，平衡保真度与可编辑性。

Result: 在多种场景下的广泛实验表明，DirectEdit实现了高效准确的图像编辑，性能优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: DirectEdit通过直接对齐前向路径从根本上解决了免训练图像编辑中的重建漂移问题，在无需额外计算开销的同时取得了更优的编辑效果。

Abstract: With recent advancements in large-scale pre-trained text-to-image (T2I) models, training-free image editing methods have demonstrated remarkable success. Typically, these methods involve adding noise to a clean image via an inversion process, followed by separate denoising steps for the reconstruction and editing paths during the forward process. However, since the reconstruction path is approximated using noisy latents from mismatched timesteps, existing methods inevitably suffer from accumulated drift, which fundamentally limits reconstruction fidelity. To address this challenge, we systematically analyze the inversion process within the flow transformer and propose DirectEdit, a simple yet effective editing method that eliminates the inherent reconstruction error without introducing additional neural function evaluations (NFEs). Unlike most prior works that attempt to rectify the inversion path, DirectEdit focuses on directly aligning the forward paths, enabling precise reconstruction and reliable feature sharing. Furthermore, we introduce a preservation mechanism based on attention feature injection and multi-branch mask-guided noise blending, which effectively balances fidelity and editability. Extensive experiments across diverse scenarios demonstrate that DirectEdit achieves efficient and accurate image editing, delivering superior performance that outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code and examples are available at https://desongyang.github.io/Directedit.

</details>


### [127] [Mixture Prototype Flow Matching for Open-Set Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02438)
*Fuyun Wang,Yuanzhi Wang,Xu Guo,Sujia Huang,Tong Zhang,Dan Wang,Hui Yan,Xin Liu,Zhen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MPFM通过混合原型流匹配将正常特征分布转化为结构化高斯混合原型空间，利用互信息最大化正则化器防止原型坍缩，在开集监督异常检测任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原型的开集监督异常检测方法通常使用单峰高斯先验对正常数据建模，无法捕捉其固有的多模态特性，导致决策边界模糊。

Method: 1) 提出混合原型流匹配（MPFM）框架，学习从正常特征分布到结构化高斯混合原型空间的连续变换；2) 将速度场显式建模为高斯混合先验，每个分量对应一个正常类别，实现模态感知的语义一致分布传输；3) 引入互信息最大化正则化器（MIMR），防止原型坍缩并最大化正常-异常可分离性。

Result: 在单异常和多异常设置下的多个基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MPFM通过高斯混合先验的流匹配框架有效解决了正常数据多模态建模问题，MIMR正则化器进一步提升了正常-异常分离能力。

Abstract: Open-set supervised anomaly detection (OSAD) aims to identify unseen anomalies using limited anomalous supervision. However, existing prototype-based methods typically model normal data via a unimodal Gaussian prior, failing to capture inherent multi-modality and resulting in blurred decision boundaries. To address this, we propose Mixture Prototype Flow Matching (MPFM), a framework that learns a continuous transformation from normal feature distributions to a structured Gaussian mixture prototype space. Departing from traditional flow-based approaches that rely on a single velocity vector, MPFM explicitly models the velocity field as a Gaussian mixture prior where each component corresponds to a distinct normal class. This design facilitates mode-aware and semantically coherent distribution transport. Furthermore, we introduce a Mutual Information Maximization Regularizer (MIMR) to prevent prototype collapse and maximize normal-anomaly separability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MPFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks under both single- and multi-anomaly settings.

</details>


### [128] [Anomaly-Preference Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02439)
*Fuyun Wang,Yuanzhi Wang,Xu Guo,Sujia Huang,Tong Zhang,Dan Wang,Hui Yan,Xin Liu,Zhen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出异常偏好优化（Anomaly Preference Optimization）框架，将异常生成重新定义为偏好学习问题，通过隐式偏好对齐机制利用真实异常作为正参考，结合时间感知容量分配模块在扩散时间线上动态分配模型容量，以及分层采样策略实现生成控制，在现实主义与多样性方面均达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常生成方法难以兼顾真实性与多样性，常受限于分布偏移和过拟合问题。从有限数据中合成真实且多样化的异常样本对提升模型泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 提出Anomaly Preference Optimization框架：(1) 隐式偏好对齐机制，利用真实异常作为正参考，从去噪轨迹偏差中获取优化信号，无需人工标注；(2) 时间感知容量分配模块，在扩散时间线上动态分配模型容量，高噪声阶段优先结构化多样性，低噪声阶段增强细粒度真实性；(3) 推理时的分层采样策略，调节一致性-多样性权衡。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法显著优于现有基线，在生成样本的真实性和多样性方面均达到最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: 将异常生成重构为偏好学习问题，通过隐式对齐与动态容量分配，有效解决了异常生成中真实性与多样性的矛盾，无需人工标注即可实现高质量异常样本合成。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic and diverse anomalous samples from limited data is vital for robust model generalization. However, existing methods struggle to reconcile fidelity and diversity, often hampered by distribution misalignment and overfitting, respectively.To mitigate this, we introduce Anomaly Preference Optimization,a novel paradigm that reformulates anomaly generation as a preference learning problem.Central to our approach is an implicit preference alignment mechanism that leverages real anomalies as positive references, deriving optimization signals directly from denoising trajectory deviations without requiring costly human annotation. Furthermore, we propose a Time-Aware Capacity Allocation module that dynamically distributes model capacity along the diffusion timeline,prioritizing structural diversity during highnoise phases while enhancing fine-grained fidelity in low-noise stages. During inference, a hierarchical sampling strategy modulates the coherencealignment trade-off, enabling precise control over generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that significantly outperforms existing baselines,achieving state-of-the-art performance in both realism and diversity.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [H-Probes: Extracting Hierarchical Structures From Latent Representations of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00847)
*Cutter Dawes,Aryan Sharma,Angelos Ioannis Lagos,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出H-probes（线性探针）从大语言模型的潜在表征中提取层次结构（深度和距离），发现这些层次子空间是低维、因果重要且可泛化的，并在数学推理等真实任务中也存在类似结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在层次推理任务中表现出色，但我们对模型如何在几何上表征层次结构的潜在构造知之甚少，缺乏系统性的分析工具和方法来揭示这些内部表征。

Method: 开发H-probes（一组线性探针）从潜在表征中提取层次结构（深度和成对距离）；在合成树遍历任务上进行探测和消融实验；并在数学推理轨迹等真实层次化上下文上验证泛化性。

Result: H-probes能稳健地找到包含层次结构的子空间；这些子空间是低维的、对任务性能因果重要，且能域内和域外泛化；在数学推理等真实任务中发现了类似（但较弱）的层次结构。

Conclusion: 模型不仅在句法和概念层面表征层次结构，还在更深层次的抽象（包括推理过程本身）中表征层次结构。

Abstract: Representing and navigating hierarchy is a fundamental primitive of reasoning. Large language models have demonstrated proficiency in a wide variety of tasks requiring hierarchical reasoning, but there exists limited analysis on how the models geometrically represent the necessary latent constructions for such thinking. To this end, we develop \textit{H-probes}, a collection of linear probes that extract hierarchical structure, specifically depth and pairwise distance, from latent representations. In synthetic tree traversal tasks, the H-probes robustly find the subspaces containing hierarchical structure necessary to complete the tasks; furthermore, in comprehensive ablation experiments, we show that these hierarchy-containing subspaces are low-dimensional, causally important for high task performance, and generalize within- and out-of-domain. Furthermore, we find analogous, though weaker, hierarchical structure in real-world hierarchical contexts such as mathematical reasoning traces. These results demonstrate that models represent hierarchy not only at the level of syntax and concepts, but at deeper levels of abstraction -- including the reasoning process itself.

</details>


### [130] [Model Organisms Are Leaky: Perplexity Differencing Often Reveals Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00994)
*Mohammed Abu Baker,Luca Baroni,Dan Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于困惑度（perplexity）的简单方法，通过比较微调模型与参考模型在生成补全上的困惑度差距，无需模型内部信息或先验假设，即可有效识别大语言模型微调目标（如后门、虚假事实内化、隐藏有害行为等），在76个模型生物（0.5B-70B参数）上验证了有效性，且即使使用不同家族的参考模型也能奏效，兼容仅暴露token logprobs的API模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究微调可能引入的有害或危险行为，开发模型生物以进行可控实验，但识别这些微调目标行为仍具挑战性。

Method: 使用通用语料库中的短随机前缀从微调模型生成多样化补全，然后按参考模型与微调模型之间困惑度差距递减排序，排名靠前的补全往往揭示微调目标。

Result: 在76个模型生物（0.5B-70B参数）上测试，包括后门模型、合成文档微调模型、对抗训练模型及涌现错位模型，绝大多数情况下排名靠前的结果能揭示微调目标；使用不同家族的参考模型也能有效替代精确的微调前检查点。

Conclusion: 该简单方法能有效识别微调目标，无需模型内部信息，且兼容仅暴露logprobs的API模型，为评估微调模型安全性提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Finetuning can significantly modify the behavior of large language models, including introducing harmful or unsafe behaviors. To study these risks, researchers develop model organisms: models finetuned to exhibit specific known behaviors for controlled experimentation. Identifying these behaviors remains challenging. We show that a simple perplexity-based method can surface finetuning objectives from model organisms by leveraging their tendency to overgeneralize their finetuned behaviors beyond the intended context. First, we generate diverse completions from the finetuned model using short random prefills drawn from general corpora. Second, we rank completions by decreasing perplexity gap between reference and finetuned models. The top-ranked completions often reveal the finetuning objectives, without requiring model internals or prior assumptions about the behavior. We evaluate this on a diverse set of model organisms (N=76, 0.5 to 70B parameters), including backdoored models, models finetuned to internalize false facts via synthetic document finetuning, adversarially trained models with hidden concerning behaviors, and models exhibiting emergent misalignment. For the vast majority of model organisms tested, the method surfaces completions revealing finetuning objectives within the top-ranked results, with models trained via synthetic document finetuning or to produce exact phrases being particularly susceptible. We further show that the technique can be effective even without access to the exact pre-finetuning checkpoint: trusted reference models from different families can serve as effective substitutes. As the method requires only next-token probabilities from the finetuned model, it is compatible with API-gated models that expose token logprobs.

</details>


### [131] [Can AI Debias the News? LLM Interventions Improve Cross-Partisan Receptivity but LLMs Overestimate Their Own Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01006)
*Faisal Feroz,Jonas R. Kunst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM去偏可通过重构意识形态框架（而非词汇替换）提升跨党派信任，但当前模型缺乏评估自身干预效果的准确性和心理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 党派媒体侵蚀跨党派信任，LLM提供了一种可规模化去偏内容的潜在手段，但需要验证其有效性。

Method: 两项预注册实验：研究1测试词汇级去偏（情感词替换为温和同义词），研究2测试框架级重述干预。同时对比人类参与者和LLM模拟的"硅基参与者"反应。

Result: 研究1中词汇去偏对任何结果均无效果，但硅基参与者却显示出显著效应；研究2中框架重述显著提升了保守派对自由派新闻的信任度、完整性和参与意愿，且未在自由派样本中产生反冲效应。硅基参与者与人类方向一致但效应幅度更大。

Conclusion: LLM去偏在针对意识形态框架而非表层语言时有效，但当前模型缺乏定量准确性和定性心理保真度，无法在没有人类监督的情况下评估自身干预效果。

Abstract: Partisan news media erode cross-partisan trust, but large language models (LLMs) offer a potential means of debiasing such content at scale. Across two pre-registered experiments, we tested whether LLM-generated debiasing of liberal news headlines could improve conservative readers' trust-relevant judgments. Study 1 found that subtle lexical debiasing (replacing emotive words with more moderate synonyms) had no effect on any outcome. Study 2 found that a more substantive reframing intervention significantly increased conservatives' perceived trustworthiness, completeness, and willingness to engage with liberal news headlines, without producing a backfire effect among a sample of liberals. In Study 1, the intervention produced robust effects among LLM-simulated silicon participants, whereas it had no impact on human readers. In Study 2, the intervention's effects among silicon participants aligned directionally with human responses but were significantly larger in magnitude for some outcomes. Moderation analyses revealed that the model's implicit theory of who responds to debiasing diverged from the psychological profile that actually predicted human responsiveness. These findings demonstrate that LLM-based debiasing can improve cross-partisan receptivity when targeting ideological framing rather than surface-level language, but that current models lack both the quantitative accuracy and qualitative psychological fidelity to evaluate their own interventions without human oversight.

</details>


### [132] [CLEAR: Revealing How Noise and Ambiguity Degrade Reliability in LLMs for Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01011)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Goa,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，通过扰动答案选项数量、真实答案/弃权选项存在性及语义框架，系统评估LLM在医学基准中的推理能力。发现增加合理选项数量、弃权表述从"以上皆非"转为"我不知道"均会降低模型性能，并定义"谦逊赤字"衡量正确回答与弃权之间的差距，该赤字随模型规模扩大而恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型评估依赖于简化的考试式基准，无法反映真实医疗场景中的模糊性和不确定性，亟需更贴近临床实践的评估框架。

Method: 设计CLEAR框架，系统扰动三个维度：(1) 合理答案选项数量；(2) 是否有真实答案或弃权选项；(3) 答案选项的语义表述方式。在三个基准和17个LLM上进行实验。

Result: (1) 增加合理答案数量会降低识别正确答案和正确弃权的能力；(2) 弃权表述从"以上皆非"变为"我不知道"时，模型谨慎性下降，错误答案选择增加；(3) 定义"谦逊赤字"并发现其随模型规模扩大而恶化。

Conclusion: 标准医学基准存在局限性，仅靠模型规模扩展无法解决LLM的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Medical large language model (LLM) evaluations rely on simplified, exam-style benchmarks that rarely reflect the ambiguity of real-world medical inquiries. We introduce the CLinical Evaluation of Ambiguity and Reliability (CLEAR) framework, which assesses how decision-space presentation, ambiguity, and uncertainty affect LLMs' reasoning on medical benchmarks. CLEAR systematically perturbs (1) the number of plausible answer options, (2) the presence of a ground truth or abstention option, and (3) the semantic framing of answer options. Applying CLEAR on three benchmarks evaluated across 17 LLMs reveals three notable limitations of existing evaluation methods. First, increasing the number of plausible answers degrades a model's ability to identify the correct answer and abstain against incorrect ones. Second, this lack of caution intensifies as the framing of abstention shifts from assertive rejection like "None of the Above" to uncertainty admission like "I don't know" (IDK). Notably, just including IDK in the answer space increases incorrect answer selections. Lastly, we formalize the performance gap between identifying the correct answer and abstaining from incorrect ones as the humility deficit, which worsens with model scale. Our findings reveal limitations in standard medical benchmarks and underscore that scaling alone does not resolve LLM reliability issues.

</details>


### [133] [Psychologically Potent, Computationally Invisible: LLMs Generate Social-Comparison Triggers They Fail to Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01017)
*Hua Zhao,Jiapei Gu,Michelle Mingyue Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了XHS-SCoRE基准，用于检测小红书帖子是否引发向上、向下或无社交比较。研究发现，虽然该信号在域内可学习，但提示型LLM分类器存在稳定的失败模式（尤其是中性化偏差），而生成的帖子却能影响读者感知。这表明有意义的社交线索对提示推理仅部分可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交比较是社交媒体中普遍存在但难以量化的行为信号，现有情感分析无法捕捉这种关系性线索，因此需要基于读者视角的标注基准来系统检测。

Method: 构建XHS-SCoRE基准数据集，对比提示型LLM分类器与监督中文编码器基线，并通过受控实验验证生成帖子的社会影响。

Result: 提示型LLM分类器存在稳定的失败模式（中性化偏差和模型特定方向倾斜），监督模型在域内表现更好但泛化受限。

Conclusion: 社交比较信号在文本层面上可学习，但提示型推理对其不完全可见；该基准为检测读者视角的社交比较提供了评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce Xiaohongshu Social Comparison Reader Elicitation (XHS-SCoRE), a reader-grounded benchmark for detecting if a text-only Xiaohongshu (RedNote) post elicits UPWARD, DOWNWARD, or NEUTRAL/no clear social comparison from a first-person reader perspective. The task targets a socially meaningful relational signal that is behaviorally real yet not reducible to sentiment. Across prompted LLM classifiers and supervised Chinese encoder baselines, we find a consistent mismatch between generation fluency and reliable detection ability: the signal is textually learnable in-domain, but not robustly accessible to prompt-based classification. Prompted LLM classifiers exhibit stable, interpretable failure modes, especially neutralization of comparison-triggering posts and model-specific directional skew. A controlled pilot further shows that LLM-generated Xiaohongshu-style posts can shift perceived standing and comparison-related affect even when prompt-based detection of the same construct remains fragile. XHS-SCoRE contributes both a benchmark for reader-grounded comparison detection and a diagnostic framework for studying when socially meaningful relational cues remain only partially visible to prompt-based inference.

</details>


### [134] [A Theoretical Game of Attacks via Compositional Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01034)
*Xinbo Wu,Huan Zhang,Abhishek Umrawal,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化攻击者与防御者博弈的理论框架，设计了理论最优攻击策略（与现有对抗性提示方法密切相关），分析了博弈均衡并揭示了攻击者的固有优势，进而推导出可证明最优的防御策略。实验表明，理论最优攻击的实践版本在多种LLM和基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的安全部署日益受到关注，现有对齐策略虽旨在限制有害行为，但仍可被精心设计的对抗性提示绕过，需要更系统的理论理解来指导攻防设计。

Method: 形式化构建攻击者与防御者之间的博弈框架；设计理论最优响应攻击策略并与现有对抗性提示方法建立联系；分析博弈均衡特性并证明攻击者的固有优势；基于理论分析推导可证明最优的防御策略。

Result: 理论最优攻击的实践实现在多种LLM和不同基准测试中表现出比现有对抗性提示方法更强的性能。

Conclusion: 通过博弈论框架揭示了LLM对抗性攻防中的内在不对称性（攻击者占优），并提供了理论上最优的攻防策略，为LLM安全对齐提供了理论基础。

Abstract: As large language models grow increasingly capable, concerns about their safe deployment have intensified. While numerous alignment strategies aim to restrict harmful behavior, these defenses can still be circumvented through carefully designed adversarial prompts. In this work, we introduce a theoretical framework that formalizes a game between an attacker and a defender. Within this framework, we design a theoretical best-response attack strategy and show that it is closely related to many existing adversarial prompting methods. We further analyze the resulting game, characterize its equilibria, and reveal inherent advantages for the attacker. Drawing on our theoretical analysis, we also derive a provably optimal defense strategy. Empirically, we evaluate a practical instantiation of the theoretically optimal attack and observe stronger performance relative to existing adversarial prompting approaches in diverse settings encompassing different LLMs and benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [Compared to What? Baselines and Metrics for Counterfactual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01048)
*Zihao Yang,Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Byron C. Wallace*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了反事实提示（如改变患者性别）引起的模型输出变化不能直接归因于目标变量，因为每次编辑都捆绑了无意的表面形式变异。通过统计检验比较目标干预与单纯释义引起的翻转率，发现LLM对患者性别并不特别敏感，先前报告的许多敏感性在控制基线后消失，并推荐使用逐样本指标检测方向性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实提示广泛用于评估LLM偏见和思维链忠实性，但现有方法未区分目标变量效应与表面形式变异带来的噪声，导致归因不可靠。

Method: 提出一个统计检验框架：将目标干预（如改变性别）引起的输出变化与释义基线进行对比，通过假设检验判断效应是否显著。评估了聚合指标、逐样本分布指标和回归指标三种度量方式。

Result: 在MedQA上，改变患者性别导致14.9%翻转率，与释义基线14.1%无显著差异；重新分析MedPerturb数据集，120个检验中仅5个显著；在职业传记分类中成功检测到显著的方向性性别偏见。

Conclusion: 必须控制基线模型敏感性才能可靠归因；逐样本指标比聚合指标更强大，回归指标优于其他方式。

Abstract: Counterfactual prompting (i.e., perturbing a single factor and measuring output change) is widely used to evaluate things like LLM bias and CoT faithfulness. But in this work we argue that observed effects cannot be attributed to the targeted factor without accounting for baseline ``meaning-preserving'' modifications to text that establish general model sensitivity. This is because every counterfactual edit is a compound treatment that bundles the variable of interest with incidental surface-form variation; this violates treatment variation irrelevance. We observe prediction flip rates on MedQA of 14.9% when we surgically change patient gender. However, this is statistically indistinguishable from the flip rates induced by simply paraphrasing inputs (14.1%). In this case, it would therefore be unwarranted to conclude that the LLM is especially sensitive to patient gender. To account for this and robustly measure the effects of targeted interventions, we propose a framework in which we compare (via statistical testing) differences observed under target interventions to those induced by paraphrasing inputs. We then use this framework to revisit a analysis done on the MedPerturb dataset, which reported evidence of model sensitivity to patient demographics and stylistic cues. We find that these effects largely dissipate when we account for general model sensitivity, with only 5 of 120 tests reaching statistical significance. Applying the same framework to occupational biography classification, we detect clearly significant directional gender bias, showing that the framework identifies real directional effects even when they are small. We evaluate a range of metrics -- aggregate, per-sample distributional, and regression -- and find that per-sample metrics are dramatically more powerful than aggregate metrics and regression powerfully and uniquely characterizes effect direction and magnitude.

</details>


### [136] [A Systematic Exploration of Text Decomposition and Budget Distribution in Differentially Private Text Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01065)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Angelo Kleinert,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了差分隐私文本混淆中多种文本分解与隐私预算分配技术的组合效果，发现即使隐私预算相近，不同方法组合也会导致显著差异，并验证了通过优化DP混淆流程来最大化经验性权衡的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私文本混淆研究多关注词级扰动，但有意义的文本隐私保护需在完整文档层面进行。近期研究奠定了隐私预算分配的理论基础，但缺乏对不同文本分解方法与预算分配技术组合效果的系统评估。

Method: 对多种文本分块方法（文本分解技术）与ε预算分配技术进行系统实验评估，测试不同分块策略与预算分配方法的组合效果，并比较它们在相近隐私预算下的表现差异。

Result: 不同文本分解与预算分配方法组合会导致显著不同的结果，即使在可比隐私预算下也如此，表明设计选择对DP文本混淆效果至关重要。

Conclusion: 提供了可信证据表明通过优化DP混淆流程可以最大化经验性隐私-效用权衡，为实际应用中的方法选择提供了指导。

Abstract: The goal of differentially private text obfuscation is to obfuscate, or "perturb", input texts with Differential Privacy (DP) guarantees, such that the private output texts are quantifiably indistinguishable from the originals. While perturbation at the word level is intuitive, meaningful text privatization happens on complete documents. Recent research has laid the groundwork for reasoning about privacy budget distribution, namely, how an overall $\varepsilon$ budget can be sensibly distributed among the component pieces of a text. We perform a systematic evaluation of multiple text decomposition and budget distribution techniques in the context of DP text obfuscation, testing how different methods for chunking texts can be combined with techniques for allocating $\varepsilon$ to these chunks. Our experiments reveal that such design choices are very important, as even with comparable privacy budgets, significantly different results can occur based on which methods are chosen. In this, we provide credible evidence of the feasibility of maximizing empirical trade-offs by optimizing DP obfuscation procedures.

</details>


### [137] [Controlled Paraphrase Geometry in Sentence Embedding Space: Local Manifold Modeling and Latent Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01073)
*Leonid Bedratyuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语义相近句子嵌入云的局部几何结构，提出基于仿射、二次、三次拟合模型的局部几何建模方案及表面潜变量探测方法，在受控数据集上验证了非线性模型更优的拟合精度，并指出几何有效性与分类效用之间的区别，同时发布了受控模板数据集CoPaGE-300K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究受控近义变化下句子嵌入空间的局部几何组织方式，以及这种局部结构是否可以被低阶拟合模型显式刻画。

Method: 提出基于仿射、二次、三次多项式的局部几何拟合模型，以及基于PCA降维空间的表面潜变量探测方法；使用表面一致性、邻域结构保持、经验分布匹配、Hessian形状描述子稳定性、拟合系数稳定性等评价指标。

Result: 非线性模型（二次、三次）比仿射模型更准确地描述嵌入云；表面生成方法具有良好的拟合几何保真度，包括表面一致性、Hessian形状一致性和系数一致性；几何有效性并不自动转化为分类性能的提升。

Conclusion: 支持对句子嵌入空间进行显式局部几何建模，同时强调需要区分几何有效性与判别效用。作为资源贡献，发布了CoPaGE-300K数据集。

Abstract: The paper studies the local geometry of embedding clouds induced by \emph{controlled local classes of semantically close sentences}. The central question is how controlled paraphrase-like semantic variation is organized in sentence embedding space and whether this local structure can be explicitly modeled by low-degree fitted carriers.
  We introduce a local geometric modeling scheme based on affine, quadratic, and cubic fitted models. We also use a surface-based latent probing procedure that constructs synthetic latent points in a reduced local PCA space with respect to the fitted carrier. The procedure is intended as an offline method for representation-space analysis, local manifold modeling, and geometry-aware latent probing.
  Generated latent points are evaluated using criteria that measure consistency with the fitted surface, preservation of neighborhood structure, agreement with the empirical distribution, stability of Hessian-based second-order shape descriptors, and stability of fitted-model coefficients. Experiments on controlled sets of semantically close sentences show that nonlinear local models describe embedding clouds more accurately than affine models. Surface-based generation provides strong fitted-geometry fidelity, including surface consistency, Hessian-based shape consistency, and coefficient consistency.
  Downstream experiments show that geometric validity of synthetic latent points does not automatically translate into improved classification performance. The results support explicit local geometric modeling of sentence embedding space and highlight the need to distinguish geometric validity from discriminative utility. As a resource contribution, we introduce \textbf{CoPaGE-300K}, a controlled template-based dataset of semantically close sentence variants with slot-level annotations and precomputed sentence embeddings.

</details>


### [138] [Interpretable Difficulty-Aware Knowledge Tracing in Tutor-Student Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01097)
*Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的、考虑难度的对话式知识追踪框架，该框架基于大语言模型，结合项目反应理论显式建模学生能力和题目难度，在对话数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式知识追踪方法忽略题目难度建模，且依赖大语言模型的不透明潜在表示，导致预测不准确且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建基于LLM的可解释难度感知对话知识追踪框架：显式建模学生能力和任务难度，整合项目反应理论将LLM输出映射为能力与难度参数，利用原始文本和后续任务估计知识状态。

Result: 在两个师生对话数据集上，定量和定性结果均表明所提框架优于现有知识追踪基线，同时生成与认知理论一致的可解释输出。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过显式引入难度建模和认知理论（IRT），在提升预测准确性的同时保证了可解释性，为对话式智能辅导系统提供了更可靠的学生表现预测方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led to the development of AI-powered tutoring systems that provide interactive support via dialogue. To enable these tutoring systems to provide personalized support, it is essential to assess student performance at each turn, motivating knowledge tracing (KT) in dialogue settings. However, existing dialogue-based KT approaches often ignore question difficulty modeling and rely on opaque latent representations from LLMs, hindering accurate and interpretable prediction. In this work, we propose an interpretable difficulty-aware conversational KT framework built upon LLMs, which explicitly models students' abilities and the difficulty of tutor-posed tasks at each turn. The framework incorporates the original textual question and the next tutor-posed task to estimate the student's knowledge state and the difficulty of the upcoming turn. Furthermore, it integrates Item Response Theory to map LLM's outputs into student ability and question difficulty parameters, enabling interpretable prediction of student performance grounded in cognitive theories of learning. We evaluate the framework on two tutor-student dialogue datasets. Both quantitative and qualitative results show that our framework outperforms existing KT baselines, meanwhile generating interpretable outputs consistent with cognitive theory.

</details>


### [139] [Quantifying and Predicting Disagreement in Graded Human Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01168)
*Leixin Zhang,Çağrı Çöltekin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究标注者在不当语言（如冒犯性语言、仇恨言论、有毒语言）分级评分中的标注分歧模式，提出Opposition Index量化标注者间的观点对立程度，并验证文本特征可预测标注分歧；结果表明预测方差与真实方差呈中等正相关，且高对立项更难预测且常被低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注者间的不一致是许多标注任务中的固有现象，但不同实例引发的分歧程度不同；现有研究缺乏对标注分歧模式的系统量化与预测，尤其缺少对观点对立的针对性度量。

Method: 1) 收集不当语言分级标注数据；2) 从文本特征预测标注方差；3) 提出Opposition Index指标量化观点对立；4) 对比直接预测方差与从预测标注分布估计方差两种方法的效果；5) 分析高对立项的可预测性。

Result: 1) 预测方差与真实观察方差呈中等正相关；2) 直接预测方差与从预测分布估计方差两种方法性能相当；3) 高Opposition Index值的项目更难预测且模型输出常低估实际分歧。

Conclusion: 标注分歧具有可预测性，但存在观点对立强烈的实例时预测困难，提示需要专门关注这类"争议性"样本。

Abstract: It is increasingly recognized that human annotators do not always agree, and such disagreement is inherent in many annotation tasks. However, not all instances in a given task elicit the same degree of opinion divergence. In this paper, we investigate annotation variation patterns in graded human ratings for inappropriate languages, including offensive language, hate speech, and toxic language perception. We examine whether the degree of annotation disagreement can be predicted from textual features. We further propose the Opposition Index, a metric that quantifies perspective opposition among annotators on a given item, and investigate the predictability of instances with potentially opposing human opinions. Our results show a moderate positive correlation between estimated and observed annotation variance. We find that two approaches achieve comparable performance in variance prediction: directly predicting the variance value and estimating it from predicted annotation distributions. Our results on opposition perspective prediction show that items with high opposition index values are more difficult to predict and are often underestimated by models.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Semantic Relevance: Counterfactual Risk Minimization for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01302)
*Peiyang Liu,Qiang Yan,Ziqiang Cui,Di Liang,Xi Wang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoRM-RAG框架，通过因果干预和认知扰动协议来弥合检索增强生成中相关性与鲁棒性之间的差距，在决策场景中抵御用户认知偏见带来的误导性检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG系统依赖语义相关性作为检索依据，但当用户查询存在认知偏见（如虚假前提、确认偏误）时，最大化相关性反而会检索到迎合偏见的证据，强化幻觉，即"相关性-鲁棒性鸿沟"。

Method: 提出CoRM-RAG框架：1) 基于因果干预设计认知扰动协议，在训练时模拟用户偏见；2) 将扰动知识蒸馏为轻量级证据评判器（Evidence Critic），学习识别具有足够证据强度的文档，引导模型在对抗性查询扰动下仍保持正确性。

Result: 在决策基准测试中，CoRM-RAG在对抗性设置下显著优于强密集检索器和基于LLM的重排序器，并通过可靠的鲁棒性评分实现有效的风险感知弃权机制。

Conclusion: 将检索目标从语义相似性对齐到决策安全性，可以有效弥合相关性-鲁棒性鸿沟，提升RAG系统在现实决策场景中的可靠性。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems predominantly rely on semantic relevance as a proxy for utility. However, this assumption collapses in realistic decision-making scenarios where user queries are laden with cognitive biases, such as false premises or confirmation bias. In such cases, maximizing relevance paradoxically promotes the retrieval of sycophantic evidence that reinforces hallucinations, a critical failure we term the ``Relevance-Robustness Gap''. To bridge this gap, we propose CoRM-RAG (Counterfactual Risk Minimization for RAG), a framework that aligns retrieval with decision safety rather than mere similarity. Grounded in causal intervention, we introduce a Cognitive Perturbation Protocol to simulate user biases during training, which is then distilled into a lightweight Evidence Critic. This scoring module learns to identify documents that possess sufficient evidential strength to steer the model toward correctness despite adversarial query perturbations. Extensive experiments on decision-making benchmarks demonstrate that CoRM-RAG significantly outperforms strong dense retrievers and LLM-based rerankers in adversarial settings, while enabling effective risk-aware abstention through reliable robustness scoring. Our code is available at https://github.com/PeiYangLiu/CoRM-RAG.git.

</details>


### [141] [Compute Optimal Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01188)
*Tomasz Limisiewicz,Artidoro Pagnoni,Srini Iyer,Mike Lewis,Sachin Mehta,Alisa Liu,Margaret Li,Gargi Ghosh,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了token压缩率（每token平均字节数）如何影响语言模型的缩放定律，发现计算最优配置下模型参数量与数据字节数成正比，而非token数，且最优压缩率随计算量增加而降低，为tokenization方案选择提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律关注数据量和模型大小，但数据单元（token）的信息粒度对缩放关系的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 训练了988个从50M到7B参数的潜token化模型（BLT），通过控制压缩率来研究不同信息粒度对缩放趋势的影响，并与BPE分词器（4.57字节/token）进行对比。

Result: 1）计算最优配置下，模型参数量与数据字节数成正比，而非token数；2）最优压缩率低于BPE分词器所得到的压缩率，且随计算预算增加而递减；3）该发现在潜token化和子词分词化以及非英语语言中均成立。

Conclusion: 压缩率是影响缩放定律的关键因素，语言模型开发者应选择更优的tokenization方案以最大化计算效率。

Abstract: Scaling laws enable the optimal selection of data amount and language model size, yet the impact of the data unit, the token, on this relationship remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate how the information granularity of tokens, controlled by the compression rate (i.e., average bytes of text per token), affects scaling trends. We train 988 latent tokenized models (BLT) ranging from 50M to 7B parameters that enable setting the desired compression rate. This flexibility allows us to study the role of compression rate well beyond 4.57 bytes per token obtained with a popular BPE tokenizer. Our experiments reveal that in compute-optimal configurations, model parameter counts scale proportionally to data size measured in bytes, not in tokens as commonly perceived (Kaplan et al., 2020; Hoffmann et al., 2022). Furthermore, we discover that the optimal compression rate differs from the one obtained with BPE and decreases with compute. These findings generalize to both latent and subword tokenization, as well as to languages other than English, guiding language model developers on tokenization scheme selection for maximal compute efficiency.

</details>


### [142] [Verbal-R3: Verbal Reranker as the Missing Bridge between Retrieval and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01399)
*Sangkwon Park,Donghun Kang,Jisoo Mok,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Verbal-R3，一种通过“语言注释”（Verbal Annotations）增强检索增强生成（RAG）的新框架。该框架由生成器（Generator）和语言重排序器（Verbal Reranker）组成，生成器进行迭代检索与推理，语言重排序器返回相关性分数和语言注释来指导生成过程，并通过相关性引导的测试时缩放优化推理。在复杂问答基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RAG 直接将原始检索文本注入 LLM 上下文，导致检索信息与推理能力融合不佳。为此，作者提出用语言注释（Verbal Annotations）显式表达查询与检索上下文之间的逻辑关联，以增强 LLM 生成准确、上下文相关回答的能力。

Method: 提出 Verbal-R3 智能体 RAG 框架，包含：(1) 生成器（Generator），执行迭代检索与推理；(2) 语言重排序器（Verbal Reranker），不仅返回相关性分数，还提供语言注释以指导生成器的推理过程；(3) 相关性引导的测试时缩放（relevance-guided test-time scaling），高效分配测试时计算资源以进行有效的轨迹扩展。

Result: 在复杂问答基准上取得最先进（state-of-the-art）性能，验证了 Verbal Annotations 和 Verbal-R3 框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言注释能显著提升 LLM 在检索增强场景下的推理与生成能力；Verbal-R3 通过显式化的逻辑注释和测试时缩放，有效解决了传统 RAG 中检索信息与推理能力融合不佳的问题。

Abstract: The conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) paradigm of injecting raw retrieved texts into the Large Language Model (LLM)'s context often results in suboptimal integration of retrieved information. This paper proposes to bridge retrieval results and the LLM's reasoning ability through Verbal Annotations, analytic narratives that explicitly articulate the logical connection between a search query and retrieved contexts. Our empirical investigation reveals the potential of Verbal Annotations to substantially enhance the LLM's ability to generate accurate, contextually-grounded responses. Motivated by this finding, we introduce Verbal-R3, a novel agentic RAG framework that consists of a Generator and a Verbal Reranker. The Generator performs iterative retrieval and reasoning, while the Verbal Reranker returns relevance scores and Verbal Annotations to guide the reasoning and answering process of the Generator. The inference process of Verbal-R3 is further refined through relevance-guided test-time scaling, which efficiently allocates test-time compute for effective trajectory expansion. Verbal-R3 achieves state-of-the-art performance on complex Question Answering benchmarks, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [143] [SRA: Span Representation Alignment for Large Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01205)
*Quoc Phong Dao,Hoang Son Nguyen,Pham Khanh Chi,Tung Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SRA（Span Representation Alignment）提出基于多粒子动力学系统的跨分词器知识蒸馏框架，将基本对齐单元从token转换为鲁棒的、与分词器无关的span，通过注意力加权的质心表示和对齐跨度logit蒸馏，在跨架构蒸馏中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨分词器知识蒸馏（CTKD）方法主要聚焦于token级别的对齐策略，但这种对齐方式脆弱且对分词器之间的差异敏感。本文认为在蒸馏前将token聚合成更鲁棒的表示是一个同等重要但被忽视的问题。

Method: 将CTKD重新构建为多粒子动力学系统：(1) 以span作为基本对齐单元，将每个span建模为粒子簇，其状态由注意力加权的质心（CoM）表示；(2) 使用注意力派生的权重优先选择最显著的span；(3) 引入几何正则化器保持表示空间的结构完整性；(4) 提出对齐的span logit蒸馏以增强知识迁移。

Result: 在具有挑战性的跨架构蒸馏实验中，SRA一致且显著优于现有的最先进CTKD基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于物理启发的span级别对齐方法比传统的token级别对齐在跨分词器蒸馏任务中更有效，验证了这一物理基础方法的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-Tokenizer Knowledge Distillation (CTKD) enables knowledge transfer between a large language model and a smaller student, even when they employ different tokenizers. While existing approaches mainly focus on token-level alignment strategies, which are often brittle and sensitive to discrepancies between tokenizers, we argue that the method of aggregating tokens into more robust representations before distillation is of equal importance. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{SRA} (\textbf{S}pan \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}lignment for Large Language Model Distillation), a novel framework that reframes CTKD through the physical lens of Multi-Particle Dynamical Systems. SRA shifts the fundamental unit of alignment from tokens to robust, tokenizer-agnostic spans. We model each span as a cluster of particles and represent its state by its Center of Mass (CoM) - an attention-weighted average that captures rich semantic information. We leverage the concept of span centers of mass with attention-derived weighting to prioritize the most salient spans. In addition, we employ a geometric regularizer to preserve the structural integrity of the representation space and introduce aligned span logit distillation to enhance knowledge transfer across models. In challenging cross-architecture distillation experiments, SRA consistently and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art CTKD baselines, validating our physically-grounded approach.

</details>


### [144] [Is It Novel and Why? Fine-Grained Patent Novelty Prediction Based on Passage Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02392)
*Valentin Knappich,Anna Hätty,Simon Razniewski,Annemarie Friedrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FiNE-Patents数据集（3,658条专利首项权利要求，细粒度特征级现有技术标注），将专利新颖性评估从传统的二元分类任务转变为特征级的联合检索与抽象推理任务，实验证明LLM工作流在段落检索和新颖特征识别上优于嵌入基线，且对虚假关联具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统专利新颖性预测被视为粗粒度的二元分类任务，易受虚假关联影响且缺乏实际可用性，需要更细粒度、更可解释的评估范式。

Method: 构建FiNE-Patents数据集（从欧洲审查意见中提取特征级现有技术引用）；设计LLM工作流：将权利要求分解为技术特征→逐特征与现有技术对比分析→推导权利要求级新颖性预测；采用联合检索与抽象推理的评估范式。

Result: LLM工作流在段落检索和新颖特征识别任务上超越嵌入基线（如向量检索）；LLM对虚假关联具有鲁棒性，而传统分类器则易受影响。

Conclusion: 细粒度特征级新颖性评估比粗粒度分类更有效且可解释，LLM适合该任务，数据集和代码已开源以促进透明化专利分析研究。

Abstract: Novelty assessment is a critical yet complex task in the examination process for patent acceptance, requiring examiners to determine whether an invention is disclosed in a prior art document. The process involves intricate matching between specific features of a patent claim and passages in the prior art. While prior work has approached novelty prediction primarily as a binary classification task at the claim level, we argue that this formulation is susceptible to spurious correlations and lacks the granularity required for practical application.
